"error", message: 'Fungsi "' + funcName + '" tidak ditemukan di server Apps Script' })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON); } } catch (err) { return default khusus if (sheetName === SHEET_LAPORAN_TEMPLATE) return _ensureLaporanTemplateSheet(); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName); var header = pasang header resmi (data tidak diubah) sheet.appendRow(header); } return sheet; } // [FIX-1] Resolver nama sheet untuk export lama (case-insensitive + alias modul). // Memastikan export selalu } // HELPER WAKTU ZONA LOKAL (Spreadsheet TimeZone = Asia/Jakarta / WIB) // Digunakan agar tanggal & jam yang tersimpan SELALU konsisten dengan zona // lokal pengguna, bukan waktu UTC yang bisa "lompat" sehari di jam 00:00-07:00. function ssDateStr(ss, d) { return Utilities.formatDate(d || new Date(), ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd"); } function ssTimeStr(ss, d) { return Utilities.formatDate(d || new Date(), ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "HH:mm:ss"); } function ssNowISO(ss, d) { return Utilities.formatDate(d || new Date(), ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss"); } // ===== [v3.25.0] FORMAT WAKTU ABSENSI ===== // Sheet sering mengubah string "yyyy-MM-dd"/"HH:mm:ss" menjadi nilai Date (tanggal // dan waktu-saja dengan epoch 1899-12-30) sehingga tampilan/laporan memperlihatkan // teks mentah seperti "2026-10-13T17:00:00.000Z" / "1899-12-30T09:24:22.000Z". // Kedua helper ini memaksa output manusiawi: tanggal -> "Senin, 13 Oktober 2026", // jam -> "09:24" (nilai waktu-saja ditampilkan sebagai jam, tanpa tanggal 1899). var _HARI_ID = ["Minggu", "Senin", "Selasa", "Rabu", "Kamis", "Jumat", "Sabtu"]; var _BULAN_ID = ["Januari", "Februari", "Maret", "April", "Mei", "Juni", "Juli", "Agustus", "September", "Oktober", "November", "Desember"]; function _tanggalPanjangID_(th, bl, tg) { var d = new Date(parseInt(th, 10), parseInt(bl, 10) - 1, parseInt(tg, 10)); return _HARI_ID[d.getDay()] + ", " + parseInt(tg, 10) + " " + _BULAN_ID[parseInt(bl, 10) - 1] + " " + th; } function _absenTanggal_(ss, v) { try { if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") return ""; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]") { var s = Utilities.formatDate(v, ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd"); if (s.substring(0, 4) === "1899") return ""; // nilai jam-saja (epoch Sheets) return _tanggalPanjangID_(s.substring(0, 4), s.substring(5, 7), s.substring(8, 10)); } var st = String(v).trim(); if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(st)) return _tanggalPanjangID_(st.substring(0, 4), st.substring(5, 7), st.substring(8, 10)); return st; } catch (eT) { return String(v == null ? "" : v); } } function _absenJam_(ss, v) { try { if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") return ""; if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]") return Utilities.formatDate(v, ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "HH:mm"); var st = String(v).trim(); if (/^\d{2}:\d{2}/.test(st)) return st.substring(0, 5); // "07:32:15" -> "07:32" if (st.indexOf("T") !== -1 && /^\d{2}:\d{2}/.test(st.split("T")[1])) return st.split("T")[1].substring(0, 5); return st; } catch (eJ) { return String(v == null ? "" : v); } } // [v3.25.0] Kolom 11 sheet absensi = radius_m (jarak dari titik pangkalan saat absen) function _ensureKolomAbsen_(sheet) { try { if (sheet.getLastRow() < 1) { sheet.appendRow(SHEET_HEADERS[SHEET_ABSENSI]); return; } if (sheet.getLastColumn() < 11) sheet.getRange(1, 11).setValue("radius_m"); } catch (e) {} } function getRecentRows(sheet, maxRows) { var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow(); var lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn(); if (lastRow <= 1 || lastCol < 1) return []; var startRow = 2; if (maxRows && (lastRow - 1) > maxRows) { startRow = lastRow - maxRows + 1; } var numRows = lastRow - startRow + 1; return sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, lastCol).getValues(); } // [FIX-GALERI] Normalkan SEMUA format URL Drive ke format semat (embed) paling // andal untuk : https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=ID&sz=w1600 // Menerima: lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/ID, /file/d/ID/, open?id=, uc?id=. // Bukan URL Drive dilewatkan apa adanya; data:image dikosongkan. function normalisasiUrlFotoDrive_(v) { var s = v ? String(v).trim() : ''; if (!s || s.indexOf('data:image/') === 0) return ''; if (s.indexOf('drive.google.com') === -1 && s.indexOf('googleusercontent.com') === -1) return s; var m = s.match(/\/d\/([-\w]+)/) || s.match(/[?&]id=([-\w]+)/); if (m && m[1]) return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=' + m[1] + '&sz=w1600'; return s; } function cleanImageValue(v) { var s = v ? String(v) : ""; if (s.indexOf("data:image/") === 0) return ""; return s; } function hashPassword(password) { if (!password) return ""; var digest = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, password); return digest.map(function(b) { return ('0' + (b & (0xFF)).toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join(''); } // [v3.10.2] Cari folder Drive tujuan secara TAHAN BENCANA (urutan fallback): // 1) folder khusus permintaan (customFolderId) bila diberikan // 2) folder utama konfigurasi (TARGET_DRIVE_FOLDER_ID) // 3) folder "SIAP WANAMSKA Foto" di My Drive akun pen-deploy // 4) buat folder "SIAP WANAMSKA Foto" bila belum ada // 5) My Drive langsung (paling aman, pasti bisa menampung file) // Dengan demikian unggah foto TIDAK mati total hanya karena folder lama // terhapus, dipindah, atau kehilangan izin (penyebab utama kegagalan unggah). function _driveFolderAktif_(customFolderId) { var namaCadangan = "SIAP WANAMSKA Foto"; var it = root.getFoldersByName(namaCadangan); if (it.hasNext()) return it.next(); try { return root.createFolder(namaCadangan); } catch (eB) { return root; } } catch (e2) {} return DriveApp.getRootFolder(); } function uploadImageToDrive(base64Str, prefix, customFolderId) { if (!base64Str || typeof base64Str !== 'string' || base64Str.indexOf("data:image/") !== 0) { return base64Str || ""; } try { var folder = _driveFolderAktif_(customFolderId); var split = base64Str.split(','); var contentType = split[0].match(/:(.*?);/)[1]; var byteCharacters = Utilities.base64Decode(split[1]); var extension = "jpg"; if (contentType.indexOf("png") !== -1) extension = "png"; else if (contentType.indexOf("gif") !== -1) extension = "gif"; var fileName = prefix + "_" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase() + "." + extension; var blob = Utilities.newBlob(byteCharacters, contentType, fileName); // [v3.10.2] 1x percobaan ulang bila createFile gagal sesaat (jaringan/kuota) var file = null, errTerakhir = null; for (var coba = 0; coba < 2 && !file; coba++) { try { file = folder.createFile(blob); } catch (eU) { errTerakhir = eU; Utilities.sleep(400); } } if (!file) throw (errTerakhir || new Error("createFile gagal")); // [v3.10.2] Berbagi tautan TIDAK lagi menggagalkan unggahan: bila kebijakan // akun/domain melarang sharing publik, file tetap tersimpan & dipakai. try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); } catch (eS) { Logger.log("Peringatan sharing (" + fileName + "): " + eS.toString()); } return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=' + file.getId() + '&sz=w1600'; } catch (e) { Logger.log("Gagal mengunggah foto ke Google Drive: " + e.toString()); return base64Str; } } // HELPER VALIDASI USER KHUSUS KAS function isKasAuthorizedUser(session) { if (!session) return false; if (session.role === "Admin") return true; var uid = String(session.userId).trim().toUpperCase(); } // ========================================================================= // === PERBAIKAN TEMUAN 2: OTOMATISASI FOLDER KEDAI, ATRIBUT WAJIB & PELENGKAP === // ========================================================================= function ensureSubFolder(parentFolder, folderName) { var folders = parentFolder.getFoldersByName(folderName); } } function getKedaiSubFolder(jenisAtribut) { try { var parentFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(TARGET_KEDAI_PARENT_ID); // 1. if (kasSheet && kasSheet.getLastRow() > 1) { saldoKas = kasSheet.getRange(kasSheet.getLastRow(), 7).getValue(); } } return { success: true, total_anggota: totalAnggota, hadir_hari_ini: hadirHariIni, kegiatan_terbaru: kegiatanTerbaru, saldo_kas: saldoKas }; } function loginUser(userId, password) { if (!userId || !password) return { success: false, message: 'User ID dan Password wajib diisi' }; var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_USERS); var data = _readUsersForLogin(sheet); var cleanInputUser = String(userId).trim().toLowerCase(); var userRow = null; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === cleanInputUser && String(data[i][4]).trim() === 'Aktif') { userRow = data[i]; break; } } if (!userRow) return { success: false, message: 'User tidak ditemukan atau Status tidak Aktif' }; var dbPasswordHash = String(userRow[1]).trim().toLowerCase(); // [FIX-3] Hitung hash seperlunya (hemat CPU): exact dulu, baru varian lower/upper bila gagal var isMatch = (dbPasswordHash === hashPassword(password).toLowerCase()); if (!isMatch) { var pwLower = String(password).toLowerCase(); var pwUpper = String(password).toUpperCase(); if (pwLower !== String(password)) { isMatch = (dbPasswordHash === hashPassword(pwLower).toLowerCase()); } if (!isMatch && pwUpper !== String(password) && pwUpper !== pwLower) { isMatch = (dbPasswordHash === hashPassword(pwUpper).toLowerCase()); } } if (isMatch) { var token = "token_" + userRow[0] + "_" + new Date().getTime(); var sessionData = { userId: userRow[0], name: userRow[2], role: userRow[3] }; CacheService.getScriptCache().put(token, JSON.stringify(sessionData), 21600); writeLog(userRow[0], "LOGIN", "Login berhasil menggunakan password"); // [FIX-3] Sertakan dashboard agar frontend langsung tampil tanpa request ke-2 var dash = null; try { dash = _buildDashboardForSession(sessionData); } catch (eD) { dash = null; } return { success: true, sessionToken: token, user: { user_id: userRow[0], nama_lengkap: userRow[2], role: userRow[3] }, profil_lengkap: (userRow[3] === "Alumni") ? true : _profilAnggotaLengkap_(userRow[0]), // [v3.34.0] dashboard: dash }; } else { return { success: false, message: 'Password salah' }; } } function registerPasskey(token, credentialData) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang", "Alumni"]); // [v3.17.0] rekap ke sheet passkeys/biometrik if (session.role === "Alumni" && _alumniFotoBelum_(session.userId)) return _tolakFotoBelum_(); // [v3.22.0] foto wajib sebelum biometrik var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var credId = credentialData.credentialId; var deviceName = credentialData.deviceName || "Perangkat Biometrik"; var nowStr = new Date().toISOString(); var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var existingIdx = -1; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === uId) { existingIdx = i + 1; break; } } if (existingIdx !== -1) { sheet.getRange(existingIdx, 2, 1, 3).setValues([[credId, deviceName, nowStr]]); } else { sheet.appendRow([uId, credId, deviceName, nowStr]); } writeLog(session.userId, "REGISTER_PASSKEY", "Mendaftarkan Biometrik/Passkey perangkat: " + deviceName); return { success: true, message: "Sidik jari / Biometrik perangkat berhasil didaftarkan!" }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function loginWithPasskey(userId, credentialId) { try { if (!userId || !credentialId) return { success: false, message: "Kredensial biometrik tidak valid." }; var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var pSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_PASSKEYS); if (!pSheet) return { success: false, message: "Belum ada data biometrik yang terdaftar di sistem." }; var uId = String(userId).trim().toLowerCase(); // [FIX-3] Baca passkeys hemat kolom (kolom 1-2 saja) var pLast = pSheet.getLastRow(); var pData = pLast > 1 ? pSheet.getRange(2, 1, pLast - 1, Math.min(pSheet.getLastColumn(), 4)).getValues() : []; var matchedPasskey = null; // Pastikan folder Kedai dan subfoldernya langsung dibuat try { getKedaiSubFolder("Atribut Wajib"); getKedaiSubFolder("Atribut Pelengkap"); } catch(e) {} } // [SHEETS] Periksa & buatkan SEMUA sheet yang dibutuhkan fungsi (idempoten). // Cara pakai dari editor Apps Script: jalankan repairDatabaseSchema() tanpa argumen. // Via API (khusus Admin): repairDatabaseSchema(sessionToken). // Membuat sheet + header yang hilang; mengisi akun admin default bila sheet Users kosong. function repairDatabaseSchema(token) { if (token) validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var names = [SHEET_USERS, SHEET_PROFILE, SHEET_ABSENSI, SHEET_KEGIATAN, SHEET_INVENTARIS, SHEET_PEMINJAMAN, SHEET_PASSKEYS, SHEET_KAS, SHEET_LOG, SHEET_AGENDA, SHEET_MATERI, SHEET_POTENSI, SHEET_POTENSI_SOAL, SHEET_KEDAI, SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI, SHEET_LAPORAN_TEMPLATE]; var created = []; var existed = []; for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) { var already = !!ss.getSheetByName(names[i]); ensureSheet_(names[i]); if (already) existed.push(names[i]); else created.push(names[i]); } try { getKedaiSubFolder("Atribut Wajib"); getKedaiSubFolder("Atribut Pelengkap"); } catch (eDrive) {} var seeded = false; try { var sUsers = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS); if (sUsers && sUsers.getLastRow() <= 1) { sUsers.appendRow(["admin", hashPassword("admin123"), "Administrator Utama", "Admin", "Aktif", new Date().toISOString()]); var sProfile = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_PROFILE); if (sProfile && sProfile.getLastRow() <= 1) { sProfile.appendRow(["admin", "12.34.56.78", "Administrator Utama", "Jakarta", "2000-01-01", "Laki-laki", "Pembina", "Manggala", "Markas Gerakan Pramuka", "08123456789", "", new Date().toISOString()]); } seeded = true; } } catch (eSeed) {} try { writeLog("system", "REPAIR_SCHEMA", "Perbaikan skema: dibuat [" + created.join(", ") + "]"); } catch (eL) {} return { success: true, created: created, existed: existed, adminSeeded: seeded, message: created.length ? ("Sheet dibuat: " + created.join(", ")) : "Semua sheet sudah lengkap." }; } function writeLog(userId, aksi, detail) { try { var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_LOG); var idLog = "LOG-" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(); var timestamp = new Date().toISOString(); sheet.appendRow([idLog, timestamp, userId, aksi, detail, "Client Script Engine"]); } catch(e) { Logger.log("Gagal menulis log: " + e.toString()); } } function validateSession(token, allowedRoles) { if (!token) throw new Error("Sesi kadaluarsa. Silakan login kembali."); var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache(); var sessionDataStr = cache.get(token); var session = null; if (sessionDataStr) { try { session = JSON.parse(sessionDataStr); } catch(e) { session = null; } } if (!session && token.indexOf("token_") === 0) { var parts = token.split("_"); if (parts.length >= 2) { var uId = parts[1]; var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS); if (sheet) { var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === uId.trim().toLowerCase() && String(data[i][4]).trim() === 'Aktif') { session = { userId: data[i][0], name: data[i][2], role: data[i][3] }; try { cache.put(token, JSON.stringify(session), 21600); } catch(cErr){} break; } } } } } if (!session) throw new Error("Sesi tidak valid atau telah berakhir."); if (allowedRoles && allowedRoles.indexOf(session.role) === -1) { throw new Error("Anda tidak memiliki hak akses untuk fungsi ini."); } return session; } function logoutUser(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); CacheService.getScriptCache().remove(token); writeLog(session.userId, "LOGOUT", "Keluar dari sistem"); return { success: true }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === MODUL KEDAI PENGGALANG (PERBAIKAN TEMUAN 1 & 2) === // ========================================================================= // AMBIL LIST BARANG KEDAI (DENGAN AUTO-CREATE SHEET KEDAI & DRIVE FOLDER) function getKedaiList(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); // PERBAIKAN TEMUAN 1: Auto-create sheet 'Kedai' dan 'Hasil Kedai' jika belum ada var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_KEDAI); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_KEDAI); sheet.appendRow(["ID_barang", "nama_barang", "jenis_atribut", "jumlah", "harga_satuan", "foto_barang", "keterangan"]); } var sHasil = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI); if (!sHasil) { sHasil = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI); sHasil.appendRow(["ID_barang", "nama_barang", "jenis_atribut", "jumlah", "total_pembayaran", "created_at", "created_by"]); } // PERBAIKAN TEMUAN 2: Pastikan folder Drive "Kedai" dan subfoldernya sudah eksis try { getKedaiSubFolder("Atribut Wajib"); } catch(eDrive) {} var data = getRecentRows(sheet, 500); var list = []; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var idBarang = data[i][0]; var namaBarang = data[i][1]; if (!idBarang && !namaBarang) continue; var jumlah = parseInt(data[i][3]) || 0; var hargaSatuan = parseFloat(data[i][4]) || 0; var fotoUrl = cleanImageValue(data[i][5]); var keterangan = data[i][6] || (jumlah > 0 ? "Ada" : "Habis"); list.push({ id_barang: idBarang, nama_barang: namaBarang, jenis_atribut: data[i][2] || "Atribut Wajib", jumlah: jumlah, harga_satuan: hargaSatuan, foto_barang: fotoUrl, keterangan: keterangan }); } return { success: true, list: list, isSpecialModifier: isKedaiSpecialUser(session), isAdmin: session.role === "Admin", // Flag dinamis otorisasi (dipakai frontend untuk sinkronisasi tombol) isManager: isKedaiManager(session), // setara Admin pada modul Kedai isSeller: isKedaiSeller(session) // berhak jual/kurangi stok }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // GENERATOR ID BARANG KEDAI OTOMATIS BERDASARKAN URUTAN function getKedaiNextId(token, jenisAtribut) { try { // ID barang baru hanya untuk Admin & Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638/DGW202641) var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); if (!isKedaiManager(session)) { throw new Error("Akses Ditolak: Pembuatan ID barang Kedai hanya untuk Admin dan Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638, DGW202641)."); } var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_KEDAI); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_KEDAI); sheet.appendRow(["ID_barang", "nama_barang", "jenis_atribut", "jumlah", "harga_satuan", "foto_barang", "keterangan"]); } var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var isWajib = jenisAtribut && String(jenisAtribut).trim().toLowerCase().indexOf("wajib") !== -1; var prefix = isWajib ? "KW-" : "KP-"; var targetJenis = isWajib ? "Atribut Wajib" : "Atribut Pelengkap"; var count = 0; for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { var currentJenis = String(data[i][2]).trim(); if (currentJenis.toLowerCase() === targetJenis.toLowerCase()) { count++; } } var nextNum = count + 1; var nextId = prefix + String(nextNum).padStart(3, '0'); return { success: true, nextId: nextId }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // FITUR (+) TAMBAH / UPDATE STOK BARANG KEDAI function saveKedaiBarang(token, dataBarang) { try { // Dulu khusus Admin. Kini Admin & Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638/DGW202641) // memiliki hak yang sama: tambah/update master stok kedai. var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); if (!isKedaiManager(session)) { throw new Error("Akses Ditolak: Penambahan / perubahan stok Kedai hanya dapat dilakukan oleh Admin dan Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638, DGW202641)."); } var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_KEDAI); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_KEDAI); sheet.appendRow(["ID_barang", "nama_barang", "jenis_atribut", "jumlah", "harga_satuan", "foto_barang", "keterangan"]); } var jenisAtribut = dataBarang.jenis_atribut || "Atribut Wajib"; var targetSubFolder = getKedaiSubFolder(jenisAtribut); var targetFolderId = targetSubFolder.getId(); var urlFoto = uploadImageToDrive(dataBarang.foto_barang, "KEDAI_" + (dataBarang.id_barang || "ITEM"), targetFolderId); var jumlah = parseInt(dataBarang.jumlah) || 0; var harga = parseFloat(dataBarang.harga_satuan) || 0; var keterangan = jumlah > 0 ? "Ada" : "Habis"; var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var rowIdx = -1; var idBarang = dataBarang.id_barang; if (idBarang) { for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (String(data[i][0]).trim() === String(idBarang).trim()) { rowIdx = i + 1; if (!urlFoto && data[i][5]) urlFoto = data[i][5]; break; } } } if (rowIdx === -1 && !idBarang) { var nextRes = getKedaiNextId(token, jenisAtribut); idBarang = nextRes.nextId || ("KED-" + String(sheet.getLastRow()).padStart(3, '0')); } if (rowIdx !== -1) { sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 1, 1, 7).setValues([[ idBarang, dataBarang.nama_barang, jenisAtribut, jumlah, harga, urlFoto, keterangan ]]); writeLog(session.userId, "EDIT_KEDAI", "Update barang kedai: " + dataBarang.nama_barang + " (Stok: " + jumlah + ")"); } else { sheet.appendRow([ idBarang, dataBarang.nama_barang, jenisAtribut, jumlah, harga, urlFoto, keterangan ]); writeLog(session.userId, "TAMBAH_KEDAI", "Tambah barang kedai baru: " + dataBarang.nama_barang + " (Stok: " + jumlah + ")"); } return { success: true, message: "Data barang kedai berhasil disimpan ke inventaris toko!" }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // FITUR (-) PENGURANGAN STOK BARANG & CATAT HASIL KEDAI function kurangiStokKedai(token, dataTransaksi) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); // Berhak menjual: Admin, Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638/DGW202641) dan // Petugas Jual Kedai terotorisasi (DGW20261/62/64/65). if (!isKedaiSeller(session)) { throw new Error("Akses Ditolak: Penjualan kedai hanya dapat dilakukan oleh Admin, Pengelola Kedai (DGW202638, DGW202641) serta Petugas Jual terotorisasi (DGW20261, DGW20262, DGW20264, DGW20265)."); } var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sKedai = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_KEDAI); var sHasil = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI); if (!sKedai) throw new Error("Sheet database 'Kedai' tidak ditemukan."); if (!sHasil) { sHasil = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI); sHasil.appendRow(["ID_barang", "nama_barang", "jenis_atribut", "jumlah", "total_pembayaran", "created_at", "created_by"]); } var kedaiData = sKedai.getDataRange().getValues(); var targetRow = -1; var itemObj = null; var targetId = String(dataTransaksi.id_barang).trim(); for (var i = 1; i < kedaiData.length; i++) { if (String(kedaiData[i][0]).trim() === targetId) { targetRow = i + 1; itemObj = { id_barang: kedaiData[i][0], nama_barang: kedaiData[i][1], jenis_atribut: kedaiData[i][2], jumlah: parseInt(kedaiData[i][3]) || 0, harga_satuan: parseFloat(kedaiData[i][4]) || 0 }; break; } } if (targetRow === -1 || !itemObj) { return { success: false, message: "Barang dengan ID " + targetId + " tidak ditemukan di Kedai." }; } var qtyKurang = parseInt(dataTransaksi.jumlah) || 0; if (qtyKurang <= 0) return { success: false, message: "Jumlah barang yang dibeli minimal 1 unit." }; if (qtyKurang > itemObj.jumlah) { return { success: false, message: "Stok tidak mencukupi! Sisa stok barang hanya: " + itemObj.jumlah }; } var sisaStok = itemObj.jumlah - qtyKurang; var statusKet = (sisaStok > 0) ? "Ada" : "Habis"; var totalBayar = qtyKurang * itemObj.harga_satuan; var nowStr = new Date().toISOString(); // 1. Update stok & status di Sheet 'Kedai' sKedai.getRange(targetRow, 4).setValue(sisaStok); sKedai.getRange(targetRow, 7).setValue(statusKet); // 2. Tambah baris riwayat ke Sheet 'Hasil Kedai' sHasil.appendRow([ itemObj.id_barang, itemObj.nama_barang, itemObj.jenis_atribut, qtyKurang, totalBayar, nowStr, session.userId ]); writeLog(session.userId, "JUAL_KEDAI", "Penjualan atribut: " + itemObj.nama_barang + " (" + qtyKurang + " unit) Total Rp " + totalBayar); return { success: true, message: "Transaksi berhasil! Stok diperbarui (Sisa: " + sisaStok + ") dan dicatat pada Hasil Kedai.", totalPembayaran: totalBayar }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // RIWAYAT HASIL KEDAI function getHasilKedaiList(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI); var data = getRecentRows(sheet, 500); var list = []; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { list.push({ id_barang: data[i][0], nama_barang: data[i][1], jenis_atribut: data[i][2], jumlah: data[i][3], total_pembayaran: data[i][4], created_at: data[i][5], created_by: data[i][6] }); } return { success: true, list: list.reverse() }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === MODUL INVENTARIS & PEMINJAMAN BARANG === // ========================================================================= function getInventarisList(token) { try { validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_INVENTARIS); var data = getRecentRows(sheet, 500); var list = []; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { list.push({ id_barang: data[i][0], nama_barang: data[i][1], kategori: data[i][2], jumlah: parseInt(data[i][3]) || 0, kondisi: data[i][4], locations_simpan: data[i][5], lokasi_simpan: data[i][5], tanggal_masuk: data[i][6], keterangan: data[i][7] }); } return { success: true, list: list }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function saveInventaris(token, dataBarang) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang"]); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_INVENTARIS); var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var idBarang = dataBarang.id_barang; var rowIdx = -1; if (idBarang) { for (var i = 1; i < data.length; i++) { if (data[i][0] === idBarang) { rowIdx = i + 1; break; } } } else { idBarang = "INV-" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(); } var jml = parseInt(dataBarang.jumlah) || 0; var lok = dataBarang.locations_simpan || dataBarang.lokasi_simpan || "-"; if (rowIdx !== -1) { sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 2, 1, 8).setValues([[ dataBarang.nama_barang, dataBarang.kategori, jml, dataBarang.kondisi, lok, dataBarang.tanggal_masuk, dataBarang.keterangan, session.userId ]]); } else { sheet.appendRow([idBarang, dataBarang.nama_barang, dataBarang.kategori, jml, dataBarang.kondisi, lok, dataBarang.tanggal_masuk, dataBarang.keterangan, session.userId]); } writeLog(session.userId, "SIMPAN_INVENTARIS", "Menyimpan barang: " + dataBarang.nama_barang); Kini kegagalan TIDAK lagi diam-diam. var _gagal = function (u) { return u && String(u).indexOf("data:image/") === 0; }; var gagalOpsional = 0; if (_gagal(urlFoto2)) { gagalOpsional++; urlFoto2 = ""; } if (_gagal(urlFoto3)) { gagalOpsional++; urlFoto3 = ""; } if (_gagal(urlFoto4)) { gagalOpsional++; urlFoto4 = ""; } if (_gagal(urlFoto1)) { writeLog(session.userId, "GAGAL_UPLOAD_FOTO", "Foto utama gagal diunggah ke Drive saat menyimpan " + idKegiatan); return { success: false, message: "Foto utama gagal diunggah ke Google Drive. } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // HELPER: URAIKAN ROLE TUJUAN NOTIFIKASI (untuk penyaringan per peran) function parseNotifRoles(v) { var ALL = ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]; if (!v) return ALL; var s = String(v).trim(); if (s === "" || /^all$/i.test(s) || /semua/i.test(s) || /.*pengguna.*/i.test(s)) return ALL; var arr = []; var parts = s.split(","); for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) { var p = parts[i].trim(); for (var j = 0; j < ALL.length; j++) { if (p.toLowerCase() === ALL[j].toLowerCase() && arr.indexOf(ALL[j]) === -1) { arr.push(ALL[j]); } } } return arr.length ? arr : ALL; } // ========================================================================= // === NOTIFIKASI LONCENG & DEVICE PUSH ALERTS === // ========================================================================= // ========================================================================= function getNotificationList(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); String(kasData[i][1]).substring(0, 10) : ""), module: "Kas", title: "Transaksi Kas: " + kasData[i][3], detail: kasData[i][2] + " Rp " + Number(kasData[i][4]).toLocaleString('id-ID') + " (" + kasData[i][5] + ")", timestamp: kasData[i][1] || new Date().toISOString(), roles: ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang"], type: "kas" }); } } // [v3.8.0] Notifikasi penugasan dihapus bersama fiturnya. var filtered = notifications.filter(function(notif) { var notifTime = new Date(notif.timestamp).getTime(); var isWithin24Hours = !isNaN(notifTime) && (notifTime >= twentyFourHoursAgo); var isRoleAllowed = notif.roles.indexOf(session.role) !== -1; return isWithin24Hours && isRoleAllowed; }); filtered.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(b.timestamp) - new Date(a.timestamp); }); return { success: true, list: filtered.slice(0, 35) }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === MODUL AGENDA KEGIATAN === // ========================================================================= function getAgendaList(token) { try { validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); var folderId = FOLDER_MATERI_MAP[folderKey]; if (!folderId) { return { success: false, message: "Folder materi tidak ditemukan atau kunci folder tidak valid." }; } var folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId); var folderName = folder.getName(); var files = folder.getFiles(); var list = []; var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_MATERI); if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_MATERI); sheet.appendRow(["ID_MATERI", "JUDUL", "LOKASI_SIMPAN_FOLDER", "FILE_URL", "MIME_TYPE", "DATE_ADDED", "CREATED_BY"]); } var sheetData = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var existingUrls = {}; for (var i = 1; i < sheetData.length; i++) { if (sheetData[i][3]) { existingUrls[String(sheetData[i][3]).trim()] = true; } } var newRows = []; while (files.hasNext()) { var file = files.next(); var sizeKb = Math.round((file.getSize() / 1024) * 10) / 10; var sizeStr = sizeKb > 1024 ? (Math.round((sizeKb / 1024) * 10) / 10) + " MB" : sizeKb + " KB"; var downloadUrl = "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=" + file.getId(); var viewUrl = file.getUrl(); if (!existingUrls[downloadUrl] && !existingUrls[viewUrl]) { var autoId = "MAT-" + String(sheet.getLastRow() + newRows.length + 1).padStart(3, '0'); newRows.push([ autoId, file.getName(), folderName, downloadUrl, file.getMimeType(), file.getDateCreated().toISOString(), "System Sync" ]); existingUrls[downloadUrl] = true; } list.push({ id: file.getId(), name: file.getName(), mimeType: file.getMimeType(), size: sizeStr, viewUrl: viewUrl, downloadUrl: downloadUrl, created: file.getDateCreated().toISOString() }); } if (newRows.length > 0) { sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, newRows.length, 7).setValues(newRows); } writeLog(session.userId, "AKSES_MATERI", "Membuka folder materi: " + folderName); return { success: true, folderName: folderName, list: list }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function saveMateri(token, dataMateri) { // [FIX-2] Upload materi tahan "DriveApp sibuk": retry + sharing tidak menggagalkan simpan. // File yang sudah terbuat di Drive TETAP dicatat ke sheet & dikembalikan sukses. var createdFileId = ""; var createdFileName = ""; var targetFolderId = ""; try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina"]); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_MATERI); // [FIX-1] Pastikan kategori valid agar folder Drive tepat var kategori = dataMateri ? dataMateri.kategori : ""; if (!kategori || !FOLDER_MATERI_MAP[kategori]) { return { success: false, message: "Kategori materi tidak valid. Pilih salah satu kategori yang tersedia." }; } var folderId = FOLDER_MATERI_MAP[kategori]; targetFolderId = folderId; var folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId); var fileUrl = ""; var mimeType = (dataMateri && dataMateri.mimeType) || "application/pdf"; var judul = (dataMateri && dataMateri.judul ? String(dataMateri.judul).trim() : ""); if (!judul) return { success: false, message: "Judul materi wajib diisi." }; if (!dataMateri || !dataMateri.fileBase64) { return { success: false, message: "File materi wajib diunggah." }; } var split = String(dataMateri.fileBase64).split(','); var b64part = split.length > 1 ? split[1] : split[0]; var byteCharacters; try { byteCharacters = Utilities.base64Decode(b64part); } catch (eDec) { return { success: false, message: "File rusak / tidak terbaca. Silakan pilih ulang berkasnya." }; } // Batas aman Apps Script (±25MB) agar tidak timeout & terbaca sebagai error Drive if (byteCharacters.length > 25 * 1024 * 1024) { return { success: false, message: "Ukuran file terlalu besar (maks 25MB). Kecilkan/kompres dulu berkasnya." }; } var safeName = dataMateri.fileName || (judul + ".pdf"); createdFileName = safeName; var blob = Utilities.newBlob(byteCharacters, mimeType, safeName); // Buat file di Drive dengan retry (atasi "DriveApp sibuk" sesaat) var file = null; var lastErr = null; for (var attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) { try { file = folder.createFile(blob); lastErr = null; break; } catch (eCreate) { lastErr = eCreate; // Folder bisa basi saat retry: ambil ulang try { folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId); } catch (eF) {} if (attempt < 3) Utilities.sleep(attempt * 1200); } } if (!file) throw lastErr || new Error("DriveApp sibuk. Silakan coba lagi sesaat."); createdFileId = file.getId(); // Sharing dengan retry, tetapi KEGAGALAN SHARING TIDAK MENGGAGALKAN SIMPAN. // File tetap ada & tercatat; berbagi tautan bisa diperbaiki otomatis saat dibaca. var shared = false; for (var s = 1; s <= 2; s++) { try { file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); shared = true; break; } catch (eShare) { if (s < 2) Utilities.sleep(1000); } } if (!shared) { try { Logger.log("saveMateri: setSharing gagal, lanjut simpan. fileId=" + createdFileId); } catch (eL) {} } fileUrl = "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=" + createdFileId; var nowStr = new Date().toISOString(); // ID Materi otomatis & selalu unik (hindari duplikat saat banyak baris) var idMateri = "MAT-" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(); var folderName = ""; try { folderName = folder.getName(); } catch (eN) { folderName = kategori; } sheet.appendRow([idMateri, judul, folderName, fileUrl, mimeType, nowStr, session.userId]); writeLog(session.userId, "TAMBAH_MATERI", "Menambahkan materi: " + judul); return { success: true, message: "Materi berhasil diunggah ke Google Drive.", fileId: createdFileId }; } catch (err) { // Jika file SUDAH terbuat di Drive sebelum error (mis. gagal sharing/catatan), // tetap catat ke sheet lalu kembalikan SUKSES agar tidak muncul "DriveApp sibuk" // padahal file tersimpan. try { if (createdFileId) { var session2 = null; try { session2 = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina"]); } catch (eS) {} if (session2) { var sheet2 = ensureSheet_(SHEET_MATERI); var judul2 = (dataMateri && dataMateri.judul) ? String(dataMateri.judul).trim() : createdFileName; var mime2 = (dataMateri && dataMateri.mimeType) || "application/pdf"; var folderName2 = ""; try { var f2 = DriveApp.getFolderById(targetFolderId || FOLDER_MATERI_MAP[(dataMateri && dataMateri.kategori) || ""] || TARGET_MATERI_PARENT_ID); folderName2 = f2.getName(); } catch (eF2) { folderName2 = (dataMateri && dataMateri.kategori) || ""; } // Hindari duplikat: cek apakah fileId sudah tercatat var already = false; try { var vals = sheet2.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { if (String(vals[i][3] || "").indexOf(createdFileId) !== -1) { already = true; break; } } } catch (eC) {} if (!already) { var idMateri2 = "MAT-" + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(); sheet2.appendRow([idMateri2, judul2, folderName2, "https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=" + createdFileId, mime2, new Date().toISOString(), session2.userId]); try { writeLog(session2.userId, "TAMBAH_MATERI", "Menambahkan materi (pemulihan pasca-Drive sibuk): " + judul2); } catch (eW) {} } return { success: true, message: "Materi berhasil diunggah ke Google Drive.", fileId: createdFileId, recovered: true }; } } } catch (eRec) {} var msg = err ? err.toString() : "Gagal mengunggah materi."; // Terjemahkan error teknis Drive menjadi pesan ramah (tanpa mengubah alur lain) if (/driveapp|drive|timed?\s*out|busy|sibuk|rate|limit|quota|exception/i.test(msg)) { return { success: false, message: "Server Drive sibuk sesaat. File biasanya sudah tersimpan — silakan buka folder materi untuk memastikan, atau coba lagi beberapa detik.", driveBusy: true }; } return { success: false, message: msg }; } } // ========================================================================= // === MANAJEMEN DASHBOARD & ABSENSI MANDIRI === // ========================================================================= function getDashboardData(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); // [FIX-3] Logika sama persis, dipusatkan ke helper agar konsisten dengan login instan return _buildDashboardForSession(session); } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function submitAbsen(token, status, fotoSelfie, userLat, userLng, isFakeGPS) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); } var targetLat = -7.563383309402712; var targetLng = 110.83081309791396; var distance = getDistance(userLat, userLng, targetLat, targetLng); if (distance > 100) { return { success: false, message: "ABSENSI DITOLAK: Anda berada di luar area pangkalan (jarak: " + Math.round(distance) + " meter)." }; Admin/Pembina melihatnya di riwayat global. === // ========================================================================= // [v3.15.0] Nama otomatis (terkunci) utk field NAMA di Bantu Absen — dipanggil dari sheet Users // ========================================================================= // === [v3.17.0] SISTEM ALUMNI: PENDAFTARAN, PROFIL TERPISAH, USULAN === // ========================================================================= function _hitungIdAlumniBerikut_(usersData) { var maks = 0; } // Pendaftaran mandiri alumni (PUBLIK — tanpa sesi). Data = objek. function daftarAlumni(data) { try { data = data || {}; var nama = String(data.nama || "").trim(); var nta = String(data.nta || "").trim(); var tempatLahir = String(data.tempatLahir || "").trim(); var tanggalLahir = String(data.tanggalLahir || "").trim(); var jenisKelamin = String(data.jenisKelamin || "").trim(); var tahunLulus = String(data.tahunLulus || "").trim(); var alamat = String(data.alamat || "").trim(); var noHp = String(data.noHp || "").trim(); var password = String(data.password || ""); var setuju = (data.setuju === true || String(data.setuju).toLowerCase() === "ya" || data.setuju === true); if (!nama || !tanggalLahir || !jenisKelamin || !noHp) return { success: false, message: "Nama, tanggal lahir, jenis kelamin, dan No. HP wajib diisi." }; if (password.length < 6) return { success: false, message: "Password minimal 6 karakter." }; if (!setuju) return { success: false, message: "Anda wajib menyetujui pernyataan kebenaran data." }; var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var usersSheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_USERS); var usersData = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); // Syarat ketat: calon yang sudah terdata (nama + tanggal lahir sama, peran apa pun) tidak dapat mendaftar ulang var tglLahirNorm = tanggalLahir.substring(0, 10); for (var i = 1; i < usersData.length; i++) { var namaAda = String(usersData[i][2]).trim().toLowerCase(); if (namaAda && namaAda === nama.toLowerCase()) { var tglAda = usersData[i][5] ? Buka menu Profil Diri, lengkapi seluruh data + foto 3x4, sebelum dapat menyimpan/mengakses fitur lain (termasuk biometrik)." }; } function _profilLulusanLengkap_(row) { var foto = row.length > 13 ? String(row[12] || "").trim() : "") && String(row[8] || "").trim() && String(row[9] || "").trim() && foto); } // [v3.21.1] Folder foto lulusan: / (subfolder dibuat otomatis) // Semua foto alumni tersimpan DI DALAM folder induk // https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1cgsMJkuhQdDkCetmSxOR-KTjJz_7c8Ji // dengan subfolder bernama TAHUN LULUS yang diisi alumni (contoh: 2020, 2021, 2022). // [v3.29.0] Folder "SIAP WANAMSKA-Hasil Export" DI DALAM folder induk Admin; fallback root bila induk tak terjangkau function _folderExport_() { try { var induk = DriveApp.getFolderById(FOLDER_EXPORT_INDUK_ID); var it = induk.getFoldersByName(FOLDER_EXPORT_NAMA); return it.hasNext() ? profSheet.appendRow([uId, "", userData.nama_lengkap, "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", new Date().toISOString()]); writeLog(session.userId, "TAMBAH_USER", "Mendaftarkan user baru " + uId); } return { success: true, message: "User berhasil disimpan." }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function deleteUser(token, targetUserId) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var uId = targetUserId.toLowerCase(); if (uId === "admin") return { success: false, message: "Akun Super Admin utama tidak boleh dihapus!" }; var userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_USERS); var userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < userData.length; i++) { if (userData[i][0].toString().toLowerCase() === uId) { userSheet.deleteRow(i + 1); break; } } var profSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_PROFILE); var profData = profSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var j = 1; j < profData.length; j++) { if (profData[j][0].toString().toLowerCase() === uId) { profSheet.deleteRow(j + 1); break; } } writeLog(session.userId, "HAPUS_USER", "Menghapus akun " + uId); return { success: true, message: "User berhasil dihapus." }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } function getSystemLogs(token, filterUser) { try { validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ensureSheet_(SHEET_LOG); var data = getRecentRows(sheet, 500); var list = []; for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) { var show = true; if (filterUser) { if (data[i][2].toString().toLowerCase().indexOf(filterUser.toLowerCase()) === -1) show = false; } if (show) { list.push({ id_log: data[i][0], timestamp: data[i][1], user_id: data[i][2], aksi: data[i][3], detail: data[i][4], ip: data[i][5] }); } } return { success: true, list: list.reverse() }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === [v3.10.0] STATISTIK PENGGUNA (DASHBOARD ADMIN, REALTIME) === // === Sumber data = sheet "log" (modul Log Aktivitas), aksi LOGIN dan === // === LOGIN_BIOMETRIC. Pengguna ber-role Admin dikecualikan. === // === Angka utama = jumlah pengguna unik non-Admin yang pernah login === // === (kumulatif, terus bertambah). Grafik = login per hari (14 hari) === // === + garis kumulatif pengguna unik. Data dibaca langsung dari sheet === // === setiap kali diminta (tanpa cache) sehingga realtime. === // ========================================================================= function _statTglKey_(d) { var bl = ("0" + (d.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2); var tg = ("0" + d.getDate()).slice(-2); return d.getFullYear() + "-" + bl + "-" + tg; } function getStatistikPengguna(token) { try { validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); var ss = getSpreadsheet(); // 1) Kumpulkan User ID ber-role Admin untuk pengecualian var adminIds = {}; var u = getRecentRows(ensureSheet_(SHEET_USERS), 1000); for (var i = 0; i < u.length; i++) { if (String(u[i][3] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "admin") { adminIds[String(u[i][0] || "").trim().toLowerCase()] = true; } } // 2) Baca seluruh log login (realtime dari sheet log) var data = getRecentRows(ensureSheet_(SHEET_LOG), 5000); var now = new Date(); var totalLogin = 0; var unik = {}; // pengguna unik non-Admin sepanjang masa var pertama = {}; // tanggal login pertama tiap pengguna (yyyy-mm-dd) var perHariMap = {}; // jumlah login per hari for (var j = 0; j < data.length; j++) { var aksi = String(data[j][3] || "").toUpperCase(); if (aksi !== "LOGIN" && aksi !== "LOGIN_BIOMETRIC") continue; var uid = String(data[j][2] || "").trim(); if (!uid || adminIds[uid.toLowerCase()]) continue; var t = new Date(data[j][1]); if (isNaN(t.getTime())) continue; totalLogin++; var kunci = uid.toLowerCase(); unik[kunci] = true; var k = _statTglKey_(t); perHariMap[k] = (perHariMap[k] || 0) + 1; if (!pertama[kunci] || k < pertama[kunci]) pertama[kunci] = k; } // 3) Seri 14 hari: bar login/hari + garis kumulatif pengguna unik var perHari = [], kumulatif = []; for (var d = 13; d >= 0; d--) { var tgl = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() - d); var k2 = _statTglKey_(tgl); perHari.push({ tanggal: k2, jumlah: perHariMap[k2] || 0 }); var cnt = 0; for (var p in pertama) { if (pertama[p] <= k2) cnt++; } kumulatif.push({ tanggal: k2, total: cnt }); } // 4) Pengguna unik aktif 7 hari terakhir var aktif = {}; for (var j2 = 0; j2 < data.length; j2++) { var aksi2 = String(data[j2][3] || "").toUpperCase(); if (aksi2 !== "LOGIN" && aksi2 !== "LOGIN_BIOMETRIC") continue; var uid2 = String(data[j2][2] || "").trim(); if (!uid2 || adminIds[uid2.toLowerCase()]) continue; var t2 = new Date(data[j2][1]); if (isNaN(t2.getTime())) continue; if ((now.getTime() - t2.getTime()) / 86400000 <= 7) aktif[uid2.toLowerCase()] = true; } return { success: true, totalPengguna: Object.keys(unik).length, totalLogin: totalLogin, hariIni: perHariMap[_statTglKey_(now)] || 0, aktif7Hari: Object.keys(aktif).length, perHari: perHari, kumulatif: kumulatif }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === [v3.11.0] BUNDEL DASHBOARD: 1 eksekusi utk seluruh kartu dashboard === // === Menghemat 2-3 eksekusi Apps Script per pembukaan Dashboard. === // === Statistik Pengguna sengaja TIDAK diikutkan agar bundel tetap ringan === // === (<2 detik); ia dimuat terpisah & non-blocking. === // ========================================================================= function getDashboardBundle(token) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); var hasil = { success: true, dashboard: _buildDashboardForSession(session), ultah: getUcapanUlangTahun(token) }; // Hanya baca sheet potensi bila perannya memang menampilkan leaderboard if (session.role === "Penggalang" || session.role === "Dewan Penggalang") { hasil.papanSkor = getPotensiPapanSkor(token); } return hasil; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === [v3.11.0] BUNDEL AWAL GAME: status gerbang + progres 1 eksekusi === // === Sheet progres HANYA dibaca bila game sudah aktif (hemat baca). === // ========================================================================= function getPotensiAwal(token) { try { var folderStatus = getPotensiFolderStatus(token); if (!folderStatus || !folderStatus.aktif) { return { success: true, folderStatus: folderStatus, progresData: null }; } var progresData = getPotensiGameProgres(token); return { success: true, folderStatus: folderStatus, progresData: progresData }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ========================================================================= // === LAPORAN EKSPOR MAKSIMAL PDF/XLSX + TEMPLATE LAPORAN (KELOLA LAPORAN) // === Sumber data = langsung dari SHEET (hasil isian), diformat rapi. === // ========================================================================= // ========================================================================= // === TEMPLATE LAPORAN PER MODUL (KELOLA DATA LAPORAN - ADMIN) === // ------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Layout sheet "Laporan Template": // kolom = [modul, judul_laporan, sub_judul_laporan, logo_url, catatan_kaki] // - modul "__global__" : pengaturan dasar (dipakai semua modul) // - modul "agenda" dst : pengaturan khusus PER MODUL (menimpa global) // Export modul X selalu memakai template modul X (Pembina->absensi, // DGW202638/641->kedai_laporan / kedai_penjualan, dst). // ------------------------------------------------------------------------- var LAPORAN_MODUL_LABELS = { "__global__": "Global / Default (semua laporan)", "agenda": "Agenda Kegiatan", "absensi": "Absensi Mandiri", "inventaris": "Inventaris Barang", "peminjaman": "Peminjaman Barang", "kas": "Kas Pramuka", "kedai_stok": "Kedai Penggalang - Data Stok", "kedai_penjualan": "Kedai Penggalang - Riwayat Penjualan", "kedai_laporan": "Kedai Penggalang - Laporan Hasil Penjualan", "lulusan": "Profil Lulusan (Biodata Alumni)", // [v3.20.0] "cdp": "Pendaftaran CDP (Calon Dewan Penggalang)" // [v3.26.0] }; function _templateDefaultGlobal() { return { modul: "__global__", judul_laporan: "SIAP WANAMSKA", sub_judul_laporan: "Sistem Informasi Administrasi Pramuka Gugus Depan Wanamska", logo_url: LOGO_LAPORAN_TERKUNCI, // [v3.23.0] terkunci catatan_kaki: "Dokumen dihasilkan otomatis oleh Sistem Informasi Administrasi Pramuka (SIAP) WANAMSKA" }; } function _ensureLaporanTemplateSheet() { var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEET_LAPORAN_TEMPLATE); var header = ["modul", "judul_laporan", "sub_judul_laporan", "logo_url", "catatan_kaki"]; if (!sheet) { sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEET_LAPORAN_TEMPLATE); sheet.appendRow(header); var dg = _templateDefaultGlobal(); sheet.appendRow([dg.modul, dg.judul_laporan, dg.sub_judul_laporan, dg.logo_url, dg.catatan_kaki]); return sheet; } var vals = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var head0 = (vals.length && vals[0] && vals[0][0]) ? String(vals[0][0]).trim() : ""; if (head0 === "param") { // Migrasi layout lama (param/value) -> layout per modul; data lama disimpan ke __global__ var g = { judul_laporan: "SIAP WANAMSKA", sub_judul_laporan: "", logo_url: "", catatan_kaki: "" }; for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { var p = String(vals[i][0] || "").trim(); if (g.hasOwnProperty(p)) g[p] = String(vals[i][1] || "").trim(); } sheet.clear(); sheet.appendRow(header); sheet.appendRow(["__global__", g.judul_laporan, g.sub_judul_laporan, g.logo_url, g.catatan_kaki]); return sheet; } if (head0 !== "modul") { sheet.clear(); sheet.appendRow(header); var dg2 = _templateDefaultGlobal(); sheet.appendRow([dg2.modul, dg2.judul_laporan, dg2.sub_judul_laporan, dg2.logo_url, dg2.catatan_kaki]); } return sheet; } function _templateRowToObj(row) { return { modul: String(row[0] || "").trim(), judul_laporan: String(row[1] || "").trim(), sub_judul_laporan: String(row[2] || "").trim(), logo_url: String(row[3] || "").trim(), catatan_kaki: String(row[4] || "").trim() }; } function _getTemplateRow(sheet, modulKey) { var vals = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { if (String(vals[i][0] || "").trim() === modulKey) return _templateRowToObj(vals[i]); } return null; } function _setTemplateRow(sheet, modulKey, obj) { var vals = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var rowIdx = -1; for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { if (String(vals[i][0] || "").trim() === modulKey) { rowIdx = i + 1; break; } } var rowVals = [modulKey, obj.judul_laporan, obj.sub_judul_laporan, obj.logo_url, obj.catatan_kaki]; if (rowIdx !== -1) sheet.getRange(rowIdx, 1, 1, 5).setValues([rowVals]); else sheet.appendRow(rowVals); } // Baca template EFEKTIF untuk satu modul: nilai khusus modul menimpa nilai global. function _readLaporanTemplate(modul) { var sheet = _ensureLaporanTemplateSheet(); var global = _getTemplateRow(sheet, "__global__") || _templateDefaultGlobal(); var out = { judul_laporan: global.judul_laporan, sub_judul_laporan: global.sub_judul_laporan, logo_url: LOGO_LAPORAN_TERKUNCI, // [v3.23.0] logo kop DIKUNCI: emblem PRADESKA transparan catatan_kaki: global.catatan_kaki }; if (modul && modul !== "__global__") { var mod = _getTemplateRow(sheet, modul); if (mod) { if (mod.judul_laporan) out.judul_laporan = mod.judul_laporan; if (mod.sub_judul_laporan) out.sub_judul_laporan = mod.sub_judul_laporan; if (mod.logo_url) out.logo_url = mod.logo_url; if (mod.catatan_kaki) out.catatan_kaki = mod.catatan_kaki; } } return out; } // AMBIL SELURUH TEMPLATE PER MODUL (khusus Admin - halaman Kelola Laporan) function getReportTemplate(token) { try { validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); var sheet = _ensureLaporanTemplateSheet(); var vals = sheet.getDataRange().getValues(); var rows = [], seen = {}; for (var i = 1; i < vals.length; i++) { var key = String(vals[i][0] || "").trim(); if (!key) continue; seen[key] = true; rows.push(_templateRowToObj(vals[i])); } if (!seen["__global__"]) rows.push(_templateDefaultGlobal()); return { success: true, rows: rows, labels: LAPORAN_MODUL_LABELS }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // SIMPAN TEMPLATE per modul (khusus Admin) function saveReportTemplate(token, cfg) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin"]); cfg = cfg || {}; var sheet = _ensureLaporanTemplateSheet(); var modul = String(cfg.modul || "__global__").trim(); if (!LAPORAN_MODUL_LABELS[modul]) modul = "__global__"; var judul = String(cfg.judul_laporan || "").trim(); var sub = String(cfg.sub_judul_laporan || "").trim(); var kaki = String(cfg.catatan_kaki || "").trim(); var logo = ""; // [v3.23.0] logo kop DIKUNCI: emblem PRADESKA (abaikan kiriman klien; _readLaporanTemplate yang memasang) _setTemplateRow(sheet, modul, { judul_laporan: judul, sub_judul_laporan: sub, logo_url: logo, catatan_kaki: kaki }); writeLog(session.userId, "SIMPAN_TEMPLATE_LAPORAN", "Simpan template laporan modul '" + modul + "': " + (judul || "(default)")); var label = LAPORAN_MODUL_LABELS[modul] || modul; return { success: true, message: "Template '" + label + "' berhasil disimpan dan akan dipakai pada export PDF/Excel/DOC modul tersebut." }; } catch (err) { return { success: false, message: err.toString() }; } } // ================= FORMATTER & PEMBERSIH ================= function _thousand(n) { n = Math.round(Number(n) || 0); var s = String(Math.abs(n)); var out = ""; for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { out += s[i]; var rem = s.length - 1 - i; if (rem > 0 && rem % 3 === 0) out += "."; } return (n < 0 ? "-" : "") + out; } function _fmtRp(n) { return "Rp " + _thousand(n); } function _cleanCellText(v) { var s = String(v == null ? "" : v); if (s.indexOf("data:image/") === 0) return "[GAMBAR]"; return s; } function _displayDateVal(v) { var s = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim(); if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(s)) return s.substring(0, 10); return s; } function _displayDateTimeVal(v, ss) { if (v == null || String(v).trim() === "") return ""; var d = new Date(String(v)); if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(v); return Utilities.formatDate(d, ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "dd-MM-yyyy HH:mm"); } // ================= IZIN EKSPOR PER MODUL (TANPA MENGUBAH RULE USER) ===== // Aturan lama dipertahankan: // - Admin : semua laporan (agenda, absensi, inventaris, peminjaman, kas, kedai*) // - Pembina : hanya absensi // - Pengelola Kas : hanya kas (Admin & DGW20264/DGW20265) // - Pengelola Kedai : hanya kedai* (Admin & DGW202638/DGW202641 setara admin) function _allowedExportModuls(session) { var map = {}; if (session.role === "Admin") { map.agenda = 1; map.absensi = 1; map.inventaris = 1; map.peminjaman = 1; map.kas = 1; map.kedai_stok = 1; map.kedai_penjualan = 1; map.kedai_laporan = 1; map.lulusan = 1; // [v3.20.0] biodata profil lulusan return map; } if (session.role === "Pembina") { map.absensi = 1; return map; } if (isKasAuthorizedUser(session)) { map.kas = 1; } if (isKedaiManager(session)) { map.kedai_stok = 1; map.kedai_penjualan = 1; map.kedai_laporan = 1; } return map; } // ================= PENGUMPUL DATA LAPORAN DARI SHEET ==================== function _collectReport(ss, modul) { var spec = { title: "", columns: [], rows: [], summary: [], totalLabel: "", totalValue: "", note: "" }; function sh(name) { return ss.getSheetByName(name); } if (modul === "lulusan") { // [v3.20.0] return _collectLulusanSpec_(ss); } if (modul === "agenda") { var d = sh(SHEET_AGENDA) ? sh(SHEET_AGENDA).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Agenda Kegiatan"; spec.columns = ["ID", "Kegiatan / Acara", "Jenis Kegiatan", "Tanggal", "Waktu", "Penanggung Jawab", "Keterangan"]; for (var i = 1; i < d.length; i++) { var r = d[i]; if (!r[0] && !r[1]) continue; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(r[0]), _cleanCellText(r[1]), _cleanCellText(r[2]), _displayDateVal(r[3]), _cleanCellText(r[4]), _cleanCellText(r[5]), _cleanCellText(r[6])]); } spec.note = "Jumlah agenda tercatat: " + spec.rows.length; } else if (modul === "absensi") { var a = sh(SHEET_ABSENSI) ? sh(SHEET_ABSENSI).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Absensi Mandiri (Global)"; // [v3.25.0] + kolom Radius (jarak dari titik pangkalan saat absen); tanggal & jam manusiawi spec.columns = ["User ID", "Nama", "Tanggal", "Jam", "Status", "Radius", "Foto Selfie", "Metode"]; var hadir = 0, izin = 0, sakit = 0; for (var j = 1; j < a.length; j++) { var ar = a[j]; if (!ar[0] && !ar[1]) continue; var st = _cleanCellText(ar[5]); if (st === "Hadir") hadir++; else if (st === "Izin") izin++; else if (st === "Sakit") sakit++; var foto = String(ar[6] || "").trim(); var radiusR = ar.length > 10 ? String(ar[10] || "").trim() : ""; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(ar[1]), _cleanCellText(ar[2]), _absenTanggal_(ss, ar[3]), _absenJam_(ss, ar[4]), st, radiusR || "-", foto !== "" ? "Ada Foto" : "Tidak Ada", _cleanCellText(ar[7] || "Selfie")]); } spec.rows.reverse(); // terbaru di atas spec.summary.push(["Total Hadir", String(hadir)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Izin", String(izin)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Sakit", String(sakit)]); spec.note = "Periode data: seluruh isian sheet absensi."; } else if (modul === "inventaris") { var inv = sh(SHEET_INVENTARIS) ? sh(SHEET_INVENTARIS).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Buku Inventaris Barang"; spec.columns = ["ID Barang", "Nama Barang", "Kategori", "Jumlah", "Kondisi", "Lokasi Simpan", "Tanggal Masuk", "Keterangan"]; var totalItem = 0; for (var k = 1; k < inv.length; k++) { var ir = inv[k]; if (!ir[0] && !ir[1]) continue; var jml = parseInt(ir[3]) || 0; totalItem += jml; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(ir[0]), _cleanCellText(ir[1]), _cleanCellText(ir[2]), jml, _cleanCellText(ir[4]), _cleanCellText(ir[5]), _displayDateVal(ir[6]), _cleanCellText(ir[7])]); } spec.summary.push(["Jumlah Jenis Barang", String(spec.rows.length)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Unit Tersimpan", String(totalItem)]); } else if (modul === "peminjaman") { var p = sh(SHEET_PEMINJAMAN) ? sh(SHEET_PEMINJAMAN).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Peminjaman Barang Inventaris"; spec.columns = ["ID Pinjam", "ID Barang", "Nama Barang", "Jumlah", "Tgl Pinjam", "Tgl Kembali", "Nama Peminjam", "Status"]; for (var m = 1; m < p.length; m++) { var pr = p[m]; if (!pr[0]) continue; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(pr[0]), _cleanCellText(pr[1]), _cleanCellText(pr[2]), _cleanCellText(pr[3]), _displayDateVal(pr[4]), _displayDateVal(pr[5]), _cleanCellText(pr[6]), _cleanCellText(pr[7])]); } spec.rows.reverse(); // terbaru di atas spec.note = "Jumlah transaksi peminjaman tercatat: " + spec.rows.length; } else if (modul === "kas") { var k2 = sh(SHEET_KAS) ? sh(SHEET_KAS).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Buku Kas Pramuka"; spec.columns = ["ID Transaksi", "Tanggal", "Jenis", "Kategori", "Jumlah (Rp)", "Keterangan", "Saldo Berjalan (Rp)", "Dicatat Oleh"]; var totMasuk = 0, totKeluar = 0; for (var n = 1; n < k2.length; n++) { var kr = k2[n]; if (!kr[0]) continue; var jmlVal = parseFloat(kr[4]) || 0; if (kr[2] === "Pemasukan") totMasuk += jmlVal; else totKeluar += jmlVal; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(kr[0]), _displayDateVal(kr[1]), _cleanCellText(kr[2]), _cleanCellText(kr[3]), _fmtRp(jmlVal), _cleanCellText(kr[5]), _fmtRp(parseFloat(kr[6]) || 0), _cleanCellText(kr[7])]); } spec.rows.reverse(); // terbaru di atas spec.summary.push(["Total Pemasukan", _fmtRp(totMasuk)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Pengeluaran", _fmtRp(totKeluar)]); spec.summary.push(["Saldo Akhir", _fmtRp(totMasuk - totKeluar)]); } else if (modul === "kedai_stok") { var kd = sh(SHEET_KEDAI) ? sh(SHEET_KEDAI).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Data Stok Atribut Kedai Penggalang"; spec.columns = ["ID Barang", "Nama Barang", "Jenis Atribut", "Stok", "Harga Satuan (Rp)", "Keterangan"]; var totUnit = 0; for (var o = 1; o < kd.length; o++) { var od = kd[o]; if (!od[0] && !od[1]) continue; var stk = parseInt(od[3]) || 0; totUnit += stk; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(od[0]), _cleanCellText(od[1]), _cleanCellText(od[2]), stk, _fmtRp(parseFloat(od[4]) || 0), _cleanCellText(od[6] || (stk > 0 ? "Ada" : "Habis"))]); } spec.summary.push(["Jumlah Jenis Barang", String(spec.rows.length)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Unit Stok", String(totUnit)]); } else if (modul === "kedai_penjualan") { var hj = sh(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI) ? sh(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Riwayat Penjualan Kedai Penggalang (Hasil Kedai)"; spec.columns = ["ID Barang", "Nama Barang", "Jenis Atribut", "Jumlah Terjual", "Total Pembayaran (Rp)", "Waktu Transaksi", "Kasir (User)"]; var totSemua = 0, totUnitSemua = 0; for (var q = 1; q < hj.length; q++) { var hq = hj[q]; if (!hq[0] && !hq[1]) continue; var qty = parseInt(hq[3]) || 0; var totalVal = parseFloat(hq[4]) || 0; totSemua += totalVal; totUnitSemua += qty; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(hq[0]), _cleanCellText(hq[1]), _cleanCellText(hq[2]), qty, _fmtRp(totalVal), _displayDateTimeVal(hq[5], ss), _cleanCellText(hq[6])]); } spec.rows.reverse(); // terbaru di atas spec.summary.push(["Total Unit Terjual", String(totUnitSemua)]); spec.summary.push(["Grand Total Penjualan", _fmtRp(totSemua)]); } else if (modul === "kedai_laporan") { var hj2 = sh(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI) ? sh(SHEET_HASIL_KEDAI).getDataRange().getValues() : []; spec.title = "Laporan Hasil Penjualan Kedai Penggalang (Rekapitulasi)"; spec.columns = ["ID Barang", "Nama Barang", "Jenis Atribut", "Jumlah Terjual", "Total Penjualan (Rp)"]; var groups = {}, orderArr = []; for (var w = 1; w < hj2.length; w++) { var hr = hj2[w]; if (!hr[0] && !hr[1]) continue; var gid = String(hr[0]).trim() || hr[1]; if (!groups[gid]) { groups[gid] = { id: hr[0], nama: hr[1], jenis: hr[2], qty: 0, total: 0 }; orderArr.push(gid); } groups[gid].qty += parseInt(hr[3]) || 0; groups[gid].total += parseFloat(hr[4]) || 0; } var grand = 0, grandUnit = 0; for (var x = 0; x < orderArr.length; x++) { var g = groups[orderArr[x]]; spec.rows.push([_cleanCellText(g.id), _cleanCellText(g.nama), _cleanCellText(g.jenis), g.qty, _fmtRp(g.total)]); grand += g.total; grandUnit += g.qty; } spec.totalLabel = "GRAND TOTAL PENJUALAN"; spec.totalValue = _fmtRp(grand); spec.summary.push(["Jumlah Jenis Barang Terjual", String(spec.rows.length)]); spec.summary.push(["Total Unit Terjual", String(grandUnit)]); spec.summary.push(["Grand Total Penjualan", _fmtRp(grand)]); spec.note = "Rekapitulasi dikelompokkan dari sheet 'Hasil Kedai' berdasarkan ID barang."; } else { throw new Error("Modul laporan tidak dikenal: " + modul); } return spec; } // ================ TULIS LAPORAN TERFORMAT KE TEMP SHEET ================= function _writeLaporanSheet(ss, tempSheet, spec, template, tanggalStr) { var nCol = Math.max(spec.columns.length, 1); var logoInserted = false; var imgBlobSheet = null; if (template.logo_url && template.logo_url.indexOf("data:image/") === 0) { try { // [v3.23.0] logo terkunci data-URI -> decode langsung var bagian = String(template.logo_url).split(","); imgBlobSheet = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(bagian[1]), "image/png", "logo-pradeska.png"); } catch (eD) { imgBlobSheet = null; } } else if (template.logo_url && template.logo_url.indexOf("http") === 0) { try { imgBlobSheet = UrlFetchApp.fetch(template.logo_url).getBlob(); } catch (eU) { imgBlobSheet = null; } } if (imgBlobSheet) { try { var img = tempSheet.insertImage(imgBlobSheet, 1, 1); img.setWidth(70); img.setHeight(70); logoInserted = true; } catch (e) { logoInserted = false; } } var startC = logoInserted ? 2 : 1; // judul di sebelah kanan logo bila logo ada // 1) Baris judul organisasi / template if (logoInserted) tempSheet.setColumnWidth(1, 90); var row = 1; if (template.judul_laporan) { var tRange = tempSheet.getRange(row, startC, 1, nCol - startC + 1); tRange.merge(); tRange.setValue(template.judul_laporan); tRange.setFontSize(16).setFontWeight("bold").setFontColor("#5D4037") .setHorizontalAlignment("left").setVerticalAlignment("middle"); tempSheet.setRowHeight(row, 30); row++; } if (template.sub_judul_laporan) { var sRange = tempSheet.getRange(row, startC, 1, nCol - startC + 1); sRange.merge(); sRange.setValue(template.sub_judul_laporan); sRange.setFontSize(11).setFontColor("#6B5744").setFontStyle("italic") .setHorizontalAlignment("left").setVerticalAlignment("middle"); tempSheet.setRowHeight(row, 22); row++; } var metaTeks = spec.title + " | Dicetak: " + tanggalStr; var mRange = tempSheet.getRange(row, startC, 1, nCol - startC + 1); mRange.merge(); mRange.setValue(metaTeks); mRange.setFontSize(10).setFontColor("#8A6D4F").setHorizontalAlignment("left"); tempSheet.setRowHeight(row, 20); row++; // 2) Blok tabel var headerRow = row + 1; // 1 baris kosong pemisah var headRange = tempSheet.getRange(headerRow, 1, 1, nCol); headRange.setValues([spec.columns]); headRange.setFontWeight("bold").setFontColor("#FFFFFF").setBackground("#5D4037") .setHorizontalAlignment("center").setVerticalAlignment("middle").setWrapStrategy(SpreadsheetApp.WrapStrategy.CLIP); tempSheet.setRowHeight(headerRow, 24); if (spec.rows.length) { var bodyRange = tempSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, 1, spec.rows.length, nCol); bodyRange.setValues(spec.rows); bodyRange.setVerticalAlignment("middle").setWrapStrategy(SpreadsheetApp.WrapStrategy.CLIP); for (var i = 0; i < spec.rows.length; i++) { if (i % 2 === 1) tempSheet.getRange(headerRow + 1 + i, 1, 1, nCol).setBackground("#F8F2EA"); } if (spec.totalLabel) { var totRow = headerRow + 1 + spec.rows.length; if (nCol > 1) tempSheet.getRange(totRow, 1, 1, nCol - 1).merge(); tempSheet.getRange(totRow, 1).setValue(spec.totalLabel); tempSheet.getRange(totRow, 1).setFontWeight("bold").setFontColor("#FFFFFF").setBackground("#B8860B"); if (nCol > 1) tempSheet.getRange(totRow, nCol - 1).setHorizontalAlignment("right"); if (spec.totalValue !== "") { tempSheet.getRange(totRow, nCol).setValue(spec.totalValue); tempSheet.getRange(totRow, nCol).setFontWeight("bold").setFontColor("#FFFFFF").setBackground("#B8860B"); } } } // 3) Ringkasan & catatan var cur = headerRow + 1 + spec.rows.length + (spec.totalLabel ? 1 : 0) + 1; if (spec.summary.length) { var sumTitle = tempSheet.getRange(cur, 1, 1, nCol); sumTitle.merge(); sumTitle.setValue("RINGKASAN"); sumTitle.setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#E7D3B5").setHorizontalAlignment("left"); cur++; for (var s = 0; s < spec.summary.length; s++) { var lRange = tempSheet.getRange(cur, 1); lRange.setValue(spec.summary[s][0]); lRange.setFontWeight("bold"); var vRange = tempSheet.getRange(cur, 2); vRange.setValue(spec.summary[s][1]); vRange.setFontWeight("bold").setHorizontalAlignment("left"); tempSheet.getRange(cur, 1, 1, 2).setBackground("#FDF8EF"); cur++; } } if (spec.note) { cur++; var noteRange = tempSheet.getRange(cur, 1, 1, nCol); noteRange.merge(); noteRange.setValue("Catatan: " + spec.note); noteRange.setFontStyle("italic").setFontSize(9).setFontColor("#8A6D4F"); cur++; } if (template.catatan_kaki) { var footRange = tempSheet.getRange(cur, 1, 1, nCol); footRange.merge(); footRange.setValue(template.catatan_kaki); footRange.setFontStyle("italic").setFontSize(9).setFontColor("#8A6D4F") .setHorizontalAlignment("left"); footRange.setBorder(true, true, true, true, false, false); } // 4) Lebar kolom otomatis (perkiraan lebar karakter) for (var c = 1; c <= nCol; c++) { var maxLen = 10; for (var rr = 0; rr < spec.rows.length && rr < 200; rr++) { var cell = spec.rows[rr][c - 1]; if (cell != null) maxLen = Math.max(maxLen, String(cell).length); } if (c <= spec.columns.length) maxLen = Math.max(maxLen, String(spec.columns[c - 1]).length); var width = Math.min(45 + maxLen * 7.5, 300); if (width < 70) width = 70; try { tempSheet.setColumnWidth(c, Math.round(width)); } catch (e2) {} } tempSheet.setFrozenRows(0); return true; } // ====================== FUNGSI UTAMA EKSPOR LAPORAN ====================== // format: pdf | xlsx | doc (.docx via Google Document) function exportLaporan(token, req) { try { var session = validateSession(token, ["Admin", "Pembina", "Dewan Penggalang", "Penggalang"]); var modul = String((req && req.modul) || "").trim(); var format = String((req && req.format) || "pdf").trim().toLowerCase(); if (format === "docx") format = "doc"; var allowed = _allowedExportModuls(session); if (!allowed[modul]) { throw new Error("Akses Ditolak: Anda tidak berhak mengekspor laporan modul tersebut."); } if (format === "doc" && session.role !== "Admin") { throw new Error("Akses Ditolak: Export DOC hanya untuk Admin."); } var ss = getSpreadsheet(); var spec = _collectReport(ss, modul); // Template modul: Pembina -> absensi; DGW202638/641 -> kedai_laporan/kedai_penjualan; // Admin -> sesuai modul yang sedang diexport. var template = _readLaporanTemplate(modul); var tanggalStr = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone(), "dd-MM-yyyy HH:mm"); var namaFile = "Laporan_" + modul + "_" + ssDateStr(ss, new Date()); var url = ""; if (format === "doc") { var tempDoc = DocumentApp.create(namaFile);
